# AIRA — SHEET SET · VOLUME 1 (SS-01 → SS-20) — THE "SHEET METHOD" SET
### Sheet Mode (Nano Banana Pro sheets → Google Flow / Veo 3.1) · 9:16 · SILENT · loops

> **Identity placeholder:** `Aira [identity reference]` carries her likeness on the SUBJECT SHEET — never enumerate
> her face; the reference holds it. We only style her. Her constant eye colour: **warm amber-hazel.**

## ⭐ HOW EACH CONCEPT IS BUILT (SHEET METHOD — read once)
This set uses the **sheet method**: instead of one flat neutral still, the subject and the hero object are each
rendered as a **detail-rich reference SHEET** — the *same* subject / object shown from **several camera angles**
(a turnaround, NOT different poses) with **tight detail crops** — so Veo has a full 3D read of who she is and what
the object is, and the sheet doubles as the deep wardrobe / material spec. Confirmed to animate cleanly in Veo
without duplicating her.

- **SUBJECT SHEET and OBJECT SHEET are generated ONCE per concept and reused as the same image in every frame.**
  **Only the ENVIRONMENT and the VIDEO prompt change per frame** — identical to the lean standard, upgraded refs.
- **FRAME 1 carries four prompts:**
  1. **Subject sheet prompt** *(generate once · reuse in every frame · one image · plain seamless background · no
     text/labels)* — a **character ANGLE-sheet of `Aira [identity placeholder]`**: the **same single neutral standing
     pose held identically and rotated through five camera angles** (front · ¾-left · right profile · ¾-right · back)
     so it reads as one person turned around — **NOT different poses** — plus tight **wardrobe detail crops** (fabric
     weave & texture, seams & stitching, buttons/hardware, trims, the hands & nails, footwear). The focus is **very
     heavy, precise DEEP wardrobe/outfit detail**; styling only (**Outfit · Headwear · Accessories · Eye colour
     (warm amber-hazel) · Makeup · Hair · Footwear · Nails**); identity/wardrobe/hair identical across every view;
     never enumerate her face — the reference holds it.
  2. **Object sheet prompt** *(generate once · reuse in every frame · one image · plain seamless background · no
     text/labels)* — the hero object as an **ANGLE-sheet** (front · ¾ · side · rear-¾ · top) of the *same* object
     **plus tight detail crops** of its materials, mechanisms and finishes + a scale cue — **very heavy, precise
     detail** (no different "versions", just the one object rotated + close-ups).
  3. **Environment prompt** — a **SINGLE cohesive scene image** of the next stretch of the ONE place she travels
     through, **much more heavily detailed** — one continuous photographed scene, **never a multi-panel / grid /
     collage / split frame**; scene only, no subject.
  4. **Video prompt** — identity-lock (animate only gaze + soft expression) → one continuous real-time camera move
     with timed [00:00–00:02]… beats carrying **explicit per-limb articulation** (the trick/element written in here
     if any) → concept-specific real-world physics; 9:16 → framing note → **Sound (diegetic)**.
- **FRAMES 2+ carry two prompts only:** **Environment prompt** (the next stretch, its own vantage) + **Video prompt**.
  The two sheets are the constant ingredients established in Frame 1.

**Per concept:** ONE place travelled through · ONE magical element (once, in one frame) · a theme-suitable number of
TRICK SHOTS (never the element's frame) · ONE hero frame (rotated). Subject never dominates every frame — negative
space, world as co-star, at least one establishing wide / environment-dominant frame per reel. **MASTER AUDIO** sits
once per concept, outside the frames (silent reel — music added in edit). Real-time motion (no slow-mo), identity
fixed / body never frozen, one grounded-surreal signature. 9:16, loops.

**LOCKED LOOK RULES (every concept):**
- **A DELIBERATE HOOK OPENS EVERY REEL** — Frame 1's first beat `[00:00–00:02]` is a chosen scroll-stopper (from the
  HOOK LIBRARY below), the single most arresting moment; then the shot **breathes** into establishing → hero → trick
  and still loops seamlessly. A **different hook per concept** (assigned in the roadmap). Real-time, identity-safe,
  believable physics — no slow-mo.
- **MASSIVE-SCALE TREATMENT (for any giant creature / object / vista)** — when a concept features something colossal
  (a great beast, towering blooms, a huge waterfall, a vast vista), **engineer the scale so it's truly felt and
  believable:** low / worm's-eye angles; a size-reference in frame (Aira + known flora dwarfed); forms that **exceed
  the frame**; sun-occlusion + a huge cast shadow; **atmospheric depth-haze** (depth = size); **slow weighty motion**
  (great mass moves with unhurried power); real downdraft / ground-tremble / impact physics; and parallax exposing
  true depth. The creature/object SHEET carries an explicit scale reference (a tiny human silhouette + a dwarfing
  detail-crop). Lighting and angle must be perfect — scale is the believability.
- **CUTE, FLOWER-LIKE SUBJECT** — across every concept Aira reads sweet, fresh and pretty **"like a flower"**: a
  soft blooming charm, delicate joyful sweetness, a gentle radiance — even in bold or action concepts keep that
  underlying cuteness (in her styling, her expression and her light).
- **ENVIRONMENT-REACTIVE CUTE EXPRESSIONS** — her face must **react to the world and to each beat**: delight at a
  view, wide-eyed wonder at the magical element, a playful scrunch/gasp at a splash or gust, serene bliss on a calm
  beat — endearing, cute micro-reactions, a **different expression every frame**, tone-matched to what's happening.
  **Identity-safe:** only her **gaze + soft expression muscles** animate (eyes, brows, a smile, a small laugh) — the
  face is never warped or re-shaped; one smooth eye-led change per clip.
- **VIVID COLOUR** — rich, saturated, colourful grades and wardrobes; punchy and vibrant, **never faded, dull,
  muted or washed-out.** (Skin tone stays natural and figure-to-ground stays clean so vivid never turns muddy.)
- **HEAVILY-DETAILED ACCESSORIES** — earrings, hat / headpiece, sunglasses, jewellery, bag, gloves, etc. are each
  described in deep detail on the SUBJECT SHEET (material · finish · shape · how it's worn).
- **BEAUTIFUL, WELL-FITTED WARDROBE** — every outfit is **flattering and well-fitted, premium and beautiful, and
  often cute** too; tailored to the mode; constant within a concept, distinct from every other concept.
- **A DIFFERENT HAIRSTYLE every concept** — one distinct, **heavily-detailed** hairstyle per concept (never repeated
  across the set), constant within that concept.
- **VARYING HEADWEAR** — rotate headwear across the 20: some concepts a **cap**, some a **cute hat / headpiece**
  (beret, boater, sunhat, beanie, headscarf, bow, floral crown, etc.), and some **nothing at all — just her
  mind-blowing, heavily-detailed hair** as the hero. When there's no headwear, the hairstyle fully carries the look.
- **BACK & FIRST-PERSON SHOTS across the set** — deliberately spread **from-behind / over-the-shoulder** and
  **first-person POV** shots across the 20 concepts (where the beat earns it — not every frame), on top of the usual
  crop / angle / placement variety.
  - **Back shots are sheet-backed:** the turnaround already carries her **back view**, so Veo animates a from-behind
    shot from a real reference — reliable, use freely.
  - **POV is prompt-generated:** there is no POV view on the sheet, so **Veo builds the first-person shot from the
    video prompt alone** — write it explicitly (her eyeline; her own hands/gloves on the bars/reins/rail in the near
    foreground; the world rushing toward camera; her body mostly out of frame; identity carried only by the hands).

**TWO AUDIO BLOCKS (every concept — precise, perfect, beat-synced):**
1. **MASTER AUDIO** — ONE background score for the whole reel, placed once outside the frames. Self-resolving ~6s
   phrases sized to the frame count (4-frame = 24s / 5-frame = 30s), **no fade-in**, an instrument palette set to the
   concept's mood, an emotional **arc that peaks on the signature** and resolves for an invisible loop; laid UNDER
   the diegetic. SILENT reel = no spoken line/lyric (an optional wordless vocal may lift on the peak only).
2. **Per-frame Sound (diegetic)** — inside each video prompt, after the timed beats. The in-frame sound *created that
   frame*, **mapped to the beats so each cue lands on the beat it happens in** (the element/trick/impact sound on its
   own beat): `ambient/object — [environment + object SFX]; voice — [breaths, laughs, soft exclamations ONLY where a
   beat earns it — serene/breath-held beats stay minimal; never blanket-paste exclamations].`

**Length: 4–5 frames × 6s per concept** — 4 is the default; use 5 when the journey through the one place needs an
extra beat. (A 4-frame reel = 24s / 5-frame = 30s of MASTER AUDIO, sized to the frame count.)

**Stills per concept** = SUBJECT SHEET (1) + OBJECT SHEET (1) + N ENVIRONMENT stills (N = frame count) = **6–7 total**
(4-frame concept = 6 stills · 5-frame concept = 7 stills).

## ⭐ SECTION L — THE ENVIRONMENT BASE-PLATE SPEC (lighting · composition · scale · depth)
> **Why this section exists:** in the sheet method the **SUBJECT SHEET and OBJECT/CREATURE SHEET must stay clean,
> neutral, evenly-lit references on a plain seamless background** — that is what lets Veo re-light and composite her
> into any scene without breaking identity. We therefore **do NOT bake scene lighting, scale or dramatic shadow into
> the sheets.** Instead, **ALL cinematography lives in the ENVIRONMENT frame** (the actual scene = the base plate Veo
> animates into) and the **lighting MOOD lives in the look-swatch.** A perfectly-engineered environment plate is what
> makes the massiveness / depth / light read as *strikingly real* — and makes Veo's job easy.

**Sheets (subject / object / creature) — reference quality only (NOT scene light):** even, soft, wrap-around studio
light with full shadow detail; true neutral colour; plain seamless mid-grey/white background; sharp, in-focus,
consistent identity/material across every angle; object/creature sheets also state **accurate physical material
properties** (iridescence, subsurface scatter, wet specular, metal/skin finish) + the **scale-reference cue** — these
help Veo light it later without locking a scene into the plate. No mood, no atmosphere, no scene, no text.

**EVERY ENVIRONMENT PROMPT is built in this fixed order (the base-plate formula):**
1. **LENS & ANGLE first** — name the shot: e.g. *ultra-wide 18–24mm low/worm's-eye* (stretches depth, exaggerates
   scale) · *wide 35mm establishing* · *normal 50mm* · *short-telephoto 85–135mm* (compresses planes, intimate) ·
   note height (worm's-eye / low / eye-level / high / crane / overhead) and any tilt. (Angle terms early — the model
   prioritises spatial layout before texture.)
2. **COMPOSITION + RESERVED SUBJECT SPACE** — rule-of-thirds / centred / leading lines / framing; and explicitly
   **reserve the empty compositional space, the ground-contact spot, the eyeline and the horizon line where the
   subject/creature will be composited**, so a figure dropped in sits at believable scale and depth. State the
   **dominant light DIRECTION relative to that empty space** (e.g. "key from camera-left behind the empty foreground
   mark") so the composited subject is lit consistently with the plate.
3. **FOREGROUND plane** — a named near element close to lens (rock, flora, rail, spray, petals) for entry + parallax.
4. **MIDGROUND plane** — where the main action/subject will sit; the readable middle.
5. **BACKGROUND plane + ATMOSPHERE** — distant forms rendered **hazier, lower-contrast and cooler** (atmospheric /
   aerial perspective = depth = size); god-rays / mist / dust / spray / depth-haze gradient; overlap of planes.
6. **LIGHT** — named source + **direction + quality (hard/soft) + colour temperature + key-to-fill contrast ratio**
   + time of day; use **rim / backlight for figure-to-ground separation**, warm-near/cool-far for depth, sun-occlusion
   + long cast shadows for scale.
7. **PALETTE / GRADE** — the vivid-but-skin-safe colour intent, keyed to the look-swatch.
8. **TECHNICALS** — "one single cohesive photographed scene, not a panel/grid/collage; scene only, no subject; no
   text or labels; realistic textures and natural imperfections (real haze, dust, wear), not over-clean CGI."

**SCALE ENGINEERING (apply in the environments of any colossal / miniature concept):** low or worm's-eye angle;
ultra-wide lens; a **size-reference** built into the plate (a known object/flora/silhouette scaled tiny); forms that
**exceed the frame**; **sun-occlusion into a corona** + a scene-wide cast shadow where the giant will stand;
**strong atmospheric depth-haze** softening the far plane; deep **foreground→midground→background** separation for
parallax; and the reserved subject-mark placed so the surrounding forms **tower over** (or, for miniatures, dwarf)
the figure Veo drops in. Depth-haze + diminishing scale across the planes = the believability of size.

**VIDEO-PROMPT CINEMATOGRAPHY (what no still can hold — it MUST be directed in the video prompt):** because the
subject is composited and the shot MOVES, every cinematographic element that cannot be baked into any reference still
(subject sheet · object sheet · environment plate) must be written explicitly into the video prompt so Veo generates
it — and it must **agree with the plate (same lens, same light direction, same scale logic).** Direct each of these:
- **Camera** — echo the plate's **lens/focal length**, then the exact **move** (crane · dolly-in/out · orbit · track ·
  push/pull · whip-pan · rack-focus · reverse-crane), its **speed**, and the **height/angle**.
- **Subject-in-scene scale & placement** — where she sits in the composition, her **size relative to the world /
  creature / object**, her **ground-contact + eyeline landing on the reserved mark** from the plate, and how forms
  **tower over or dwarf** her (the scale relationship exists only once she's composited in — so state it here).
- **Light on the moving subject** — the **key / rim direction hitting her must match the plate's stated light**; how
  light and shadow track across her as she moves (sun-occlusion corona, rim-separation from the ground, glow-cast).
- **Depth in motion** — **rack / pull focus**, shallow-vs-deep DoF, and the **parallax** between foreground, subject
  and background as the camera moves (the plate's layers only become parallax once the camera travels).
- **Atmosphere interaction** — how she disturbs the world: **downdraft flattening flora, spray, spore/petal-lift, dust,
  hair and fabric in the wind** — the living, physical proof of scale and weight.
- **POV frames** — no still exists for these, so build them wholly in the video prompt: her eyeline, her own
  hands/gloves in the near foreground, the world rushing toward camera, identity carried by hands / a reflection.
- **Motion realism** — real-time / natural speed, motion-blur where fast, NO slow-mo; the per-limb articulation and
  identity-safe expression already specified stay intact (this layer is ADDED on top, never a replacement).

**COMPOSITIONAL VARIETY (placement · angle · crop — NEVER centred-and-full every frame):** a centred, full-body
subject in every frame reads flat and un-cinematic. Across the frames of each reel, deliberately ROTATE all three,
so **no two frames repeat the same placement + angle + crop** (governed in BOTH the plate's reserved mark and the
video prompt's stated framing):
- **PLACEMENT** — push the subject/object off-centre onto the **left-third, right-third, an edge, or centre**, balanced
  against the world's negative space (rule-of-thirds); and include at least one deliberately **SUBJECT-LESS / world-only
  frame** per reel where the beat allows (pure environment as co-star). The plate reserves the mark at that frame's
  chosen position — not always centre.
- **ANGLE / FACING** — rotate the relationship to camera every frame: **front, 45° three-quarter (left or right), side
  profile, over-the-shoulder, and back** (back shots are sheet-backed — use freely). Never the same facing twice running.
- **CROP** — vary how much of her is in frame, **especially when the camera is near**: **ECU (face/eyes), CU (to
  collarbone/chest), half-body, up-to-waist, cowboy (mid-thigh), full-body, and tiny-in-wide.** On near shots use the
  tighter crops deliberately — do NOT default to full-body. The plate reserves empty space to suit the intended crop
  (a CU plate leaves a tight background pocket; a wide plate leaves the full world). Tight ECU/CU crops stay the
  MINORITY (intimacy / transform / hero beats); wides + environment-dominant framings honour the world — but facing
  and placement still vary within them.

*Grounded in standard practice — [three-point / key-fill lighting](https://www.studiobinder.com/blog/film-lighting-techniques/),
[low-angle & forced-perspective scale](https://www.premiumbeat.com/blog/cinematography-tip-creating-the-illusion-of-scale/),
[foreground-midground-background layering](https://photographyicon.com/foreground-midground-background/),
[atmospheric/aerial perspective for depth](https://www.naturephotographers.network/articles/technical/how-to-create-three-dimensionality-in-your-landscape-photos-video/),
and [Nano Banana Pro scene-first prompting](https://www.aifreeapi.com/en/posts/nano-banana-pro-realistic-images). Content rephrased for licensing compliance.*

# SS-01 · THE GREAT SKY-BEAST (Alien-Jungle Bond) · 4 shots × 6s
**Logline:** In a lush, sunlit alien jungle of floating mountains, waterfalls and giant glowing flora, a colossal
winged sky-beast answers a distant call, descends through the canopy, and lands before Aira — she steps to it and
glides her hand over its crested head, sealing a wordless bond. Awe → wonder → tenderness → soaring joy.
**Arc (one place, explored):** SUNLIT GLADE · THE CALL (hook) → THE DESCENT (trick · low worm's-eye) → THE BOND (element · hero) → TAKE FLIGHT · WORLD REVEAL (trick · reverse crane · loop).
*One coherent alien glade throughout — the same floating peaks, giant bioluminescent flowers, hanging vines,
waterfalls and warm daylight — the beats move around this one clearing as the beast arrives, bonds and departs (the
camera and the action carry it, not travel). **Element:** bioluminescence blooming to life at her touch, Frame 3
only. **Trick shots:** low worm's-eye landing downdraft (F2) + reverse crane world-reveal (F4) — never the element's
frame. **Hero:** Frame 3. Elsewhere she stands small, the world immense around her.*
> **⚠️ SCALE IS THE WHOLE GAME:** the sky-beast must read genuinely **COLOSSAL and believable** — not a cute
> dragon. Every frame engineers scale: low / worm's-eye angles, Aira and the giant flora as a size-reference,
> wings or talons that **exceed the frame**, the sun **eclipsed** into a corona with a clearing-wide shadow,
> **atmospheric depth-haze** softening its far wingtip (depth = size), **slow weighty motion** (huge mass moves with
> unhurried power), real **downdraft / ground-tremble / impact** physics, and parallax that exposes true depth. Light
> and angle must be perfect so the enormity is *felt*.

## MASTER AUDIO (outside the frames — background music)
A 24-second custom cinematic score (4 clips × 6s) in self-resolving ~6-second phrases, trimmable at any phrase
boundary. Built on vast soaring strings, a wordless ethereal female vocal, deep taiko and frame-drum percussion, low
horns and a warm sub — epic, awed, tender, majestic. Opens (no fade-in) on a hushed high vocal over a rising string
swell. Arc: an awe-struck open across the glade, a thunderous swelling descent, a hushed tender lull on the bond,
then a triumphant soaring lift on take-off that resolves for an invisible loop. Laid UNDER the diegetic — no spoken
line; the wordless vocal lifts on the bond and the flight.

---
### FRAME 1 — SUNLIT GLADE · THE CALL (hook · sweeping shadow + flock-burst)
**Subject sheet prompt** (generate once · reuse in every frame · one image · plain seamless background · no text/labels): a clean character angle-sheet of `Aira [identity placeholder]` — the **same single neutral standing pose held identically (weight even on both feet, arms relaxed at her sides, hands open and visible) and rotated through five camera angles**, shown left-to-right on one plain seamless background: **front, three-quarter left, right profile, three-quarter right, and back** (one person turned around — NOT different poses) — plus a row of tight wardrobe detail crops along the base: the iridescent bodice weave, the layered petal-skirt hem, the luminescent botanical jewellery, the hands & nails, and an ankle-sandal close-up. Even soft daylight, identity/wardrobe/hair/styling identical across every view, no text or labels rendered. **Outfit (deep detail):** an ethereal nature-explorer couture gown — a fitted deep-emerald-and-teal bodice of finely woven iridescent thread that shifts blue-to-violet in the light, laced with a delicate corset back, over a flowing layered petal-skirt of translucent chiffon in emerald, jade and soft magenta that lifts and streams in wind; a slim woven-vine belt at the waist. **Headwear:** none — bare, hero hair. **Accessories (deep detail):** living luminescent botanical jewellery — a delicate collar and ear-vines of tiny glowing seed-pods and pressed alien petals that softly pulse, fine gold-and-jade bangles, and a single vine-ring. **Eye colour:** warm amber-hazel. **Makeup:** fresh dewy glow with a soft iridescent highlight on the cheekbones and a rose-petal lip, real skin texture, no plastic sheen. **Hair (deep detail):** long, thick, glossy chestnut waves worn loose to the waist with a centre part, several fine accent braids threaded with tiny luminescent seed-beads and one delicate alien bloom tucked above the ear, soft face-framing wisps and baby hairs. **Footwear:** woven-leather barefoot-sandals laced up the ankle. **Nails:** short soft jade with a faint pearl sheen. Vivid, saturated, true colours throughout.

**Creature sheet prompt** (the hero "object" · generate once · reuse in every frame · one image · plain seamless background · no text/labels): a clean angle-sheet of one original colossal winged sky-beast — the *same* creature rotated through five camera angles on one plain seamless background: **front, three-quarter front, side profile, rear three-quarter, and a wings-spread top view** — plus a row of tight detail crops of its features: the broad bony head-crest and hooked raptor beak; the large intelligent amber eye with a slit pupil; the vast wings, feathered along the leading edge and membraned toward the tips, iridescent deep crimson-gold shading to teal-violet; the rows of glowing bioluminescent markings tracing the crest, spine and wing-edges; the powerful clawed hind talons; the long ribboned tail with a fan-fin; and the sleek scaled-and-downy hide texture. A majestic, believable apex flyer (fully original dragon-bird design) of **colossal size** — a roughly 15–20 metre body with a wingspan wider than the glade, on the order of a large aircraft; **include a tiny human silhouette beside one view purely as a scale reference** so its enormity reads instantly, and a talon detail-crop dwarfing that silhouette. Consistent clean studio light modelling the huge forms, with soft ambient occlusion deep in the wing-folds and under the body to sell mass and volume, no text or labels rendered. Vivid, saturated, true colours.
**Environment prompt** (base-plate — lens→composition→layers→light): **ultra-wide 20mm establishing, high slow-crane vantage tilting slightly down** over a sunlit alien jungle glade, deep three-dimensional layering. **Composition:** the mossy clearing sits on the lower-third with a small clear ground-contact mark left-of-centre reserved for a tiny distant figure (deliberate world-dominant negative space of canopy and sky filling the upper two-thirds, where a vast winged shadow will later sweep); leading lines of luminous roots and a thin waterfall draw the eye from foreground to the far peaks; horizon high. **Foreground:** giant bioluminescent flowers and a spiralling fern-frond close to the lens, softly overlapping the frame edge for parallax. **Midground:** the soft mossy clearing floor threaded with glowing luminous roots and scattered person-height glowing blooms (the readable middle where the action will sit). **Background + atmosphere:** colossal floating stone islands hung with cascading vines and thin drifting waterfalls, spiralling fern-trees and distant jungle peaks under two faint moons — rendered progressively **hazier, cooler and lower-contrast** with strong atmospheric depth-haze so distance reads as scale; drifting glowing spore-lights and pollen suspended in the air. **Light:** warm hazy high daylight as a soft key from high camera-back-left, gentle god-ray shafts breaking through the canopy gap, soft fill in the shadows (moderate key-to-fill contrast), warm-near / cool-far separation. **Palette/grade:** vivid saturated emerald-and-jade greens with magenta, teal and gold accents — rich punchy colour, never dull or faded, natural skin tone preserved. one single cohesive photographed scene, not a panel/grid/collage; scene only, no subject; no text or labels; realistic organic textures and natural haze, not over-clean CGI.

**Video prompt:** keep `Aira`'s exact face/identity (bone structure, eye shape/colour, nose/lip) — animate only gaze + soft expression: **awe blooming into wide-eyed wonder**, lips parting on a caught breath, eyes lifting to the sky. WIDE establishing on an **ultra-wide 20mm (deep focus)**, **one continuous slow high crane drifting over the glade** — she composited **tiny on the reserved lower-left ground mark**, dwarfed by the towering flora with vast sky negative space above, her soft warm key from **high camera-back-left matching the plate**. [00:00–00:02] **HOOK — sweeping shadow + flock-burst:** already mid-step through the sunlit glade, a vast winged shadow sweeps across the glowing flora as a deep resonant cry rolls through the valley — a burst of small glowing creatures scatters up from the mega-flowers; she halts, RIGHT foot planted, LEFT foot arrested mid-step, torso twisting, both shoulders rising, head tilting up, GAZE snapping to the sky, eyes flying wide — the scroll-stopper. [00:02–00:04] the shot breathes wide — she stands tiny in the immense glade, RIGHT hand lifting to shade her eyes, LEFT arm loose, weight settling back onto her heels, spore-lights drifting past as she scans the canopy. [00:04–00:06] a great winged shape sweeps between the floating peaks far above and begins a long, majestic **soaring glide** down toward the clearing — wings held wide and still on the air, only an occasional slow adjusting beat, spiralling lower — its jewel-toned iridescent wings catching the sun in vivid crimson-gold and teal; she takes a step back, RIGHT foot behind, torso leaning away, gaze tracking it, a nervous wondering smile. Real-time, accurate physics: warm daylight and drifting spores; the sweeping shadow scales huge across the flora; the distant beast banks on real wing-borne arcs; deep focus with real parallax between the foreground blooms, her tiny midground figure and the haze-softened far peaks as the crane drifts, the sweeping winged shadow raking across her and the flora, her hair and petal-skirt stirring in the breeze; 9:16. (Environment-dominant — she small, negative space all around.) Sound (diegetic): ambient/object — dense alien-jungle ambience, trickling waterfalls, the far thunderous cry, a felt low wing-thud; voice — Aira's soft awed gasp.
**→ Cut (match-on-action) to Frame 2 — the sky-beast drops through the canopy and lands before her (the worm's-eye descent).**

---
### FRAME 2 — THE DESCENT (trick shot · low worm's-eye · landing downdraft)
**Environment prompt** (base-plate — lens→composition→layers→light): **ultra-wide 18mm dramatic worm's-eye, camera low on the clearing floor looking steeply up**, extreme vertical scale. **Composition:** a broad gap in the floating canopy opens dead-centre-to-upper-frame — the reserved sky-slot down which a colossal winged form will descend — with a small ground-contact mark lower-frame reserved for a tiny figure directly beneath it; the towering flora converge as leading lines toward that gap; the low sun sits just inside the canopy gap so the descending mass will **occlude it into a bright corona** and cast a clearing-wide shadow. **Foreground:** giant bioluminescent flowers and fern-fronds close to the lens, poised to be flattened outward by a downdraft, edges overlapping the frame. **Midground:** the soft mossy clearing floor with luminous roots (the reserved landing spot), hanging vines and a thin waterfall to one side. **Background + atmosphere:** the floating stone peaks and canopy framed high above, rendered **hazier, cooler and lower-contrast** with a strong broad **god-ray shaft** pouring through the gap full of suspended glowing spore-lights and drifting pollen (volumetric depth). **Light:** warm daylight hard-keyed from high through the gap (higher key-to-fill contrast for drama), rim-separating the flora, teal ambient fill in the shadows, warm-near / cool-far. **Palette/grade:** vivid saturated greens with gold light and teal shadow — rich punchy colour, never dull or faded, natural skin tone preserved. one single cohesive photographed scene, not a panel/grid/collage; scene only, no subject; no text or labels; realistic organic textures and volumetric haze, not over-clean CGI.

# SS-02 · TITAN LEAP (Super-Leap · Sunset Canyon) · 5 shots × 6s
**Logline:** A graceful super-being explodes off a canyon mesa in a Hulk-scale leap, soars a colossal arc across a
vast vivid sunset canyon — spotting her landing and winding up in mid-air — and lands with a ground-cracking impact
that blooms petals and colour, then bounds again toward the sky. Power meets elegance; enormous scale.
**Arc (one place, explored):** COILED LAUNCH (hook · the launch) → THE SOAR (her mid-air choreography) → THE IMPACT (element · hero) → THE BOUND (trick · low near-miss) → SKY APEX · WORLD REVEAL (trick · reverse crane · loop).
*One coherent sunset canyon throughout — the same vermilion mesas, turquoise river and huge magenta-gold sky — she
leaps around and through it. **Element:** a shockwave-ring + a bloom of glowing petals and colour-dust, Frame 3 only
(the hero impact). **Trick shots:** low ground-level near-miss bound (F4) + reverse crane world-reveal (F5) — never
the element's frame. **Hero:** Frame 3. She appears in all 5; F2 & F5 are environment-dominant (she tiny) so the
canyon's scale keeps landing.*
> **⚠️ SCALE + BELIEVABILITY:** her leap is superhuman (the one impossible beat) but every secondary physic is REAL —
> crouch-load → explosive extension, the ground **fracturing** with a dust-plume and debris on true ballistic arcs,
> air-drag streaming her hair and sash, a real **impact crater + shockwave**, dust settling a beat late; real-time,
> NO slow-mo. The canyon must read genuinely COLOSSAL (low angles, her tiny against mile-high walls, atmospheric
> depth-haze, parallax) so the leap-distances feel enormous. **Mid-air she is ACTIVE, never a passive pose:** she
> spots her landing, tucks/extends, tips forward and winds up for the smash — the Hulk logic, rendered elegant.

## MASTER AUDIO (outside the frames — background music)
A 30-second custom cinematic score (5 clips × 6s) in self-resolving ~6-second phrases, trimmable at any phrase
boundary. Built on pounding taiko and frame-drums, huge brass, driving strings, a fierce wordless female vocal and a
deep sub — epic, powerful, exhilarating. Opens (no fade-in) on an explosive percussive hit. Arc: a detonating launch,
a soaring string lift on the arc, a massive brass-and-drum impact-boom on the landing, a driving re-launch, then a
triumphant sky-apex swell that resolves for an invisible loop. Laid UNDER the diegetic — no spoken line; the fierce
wordless vocal lifts on the impact and the apex.

### FRAME 5 — SKY APEX · WORLD REVEAL (trick shot · reverse crane reveal · loop)
**Environment prompt** (base-plate — lens→composition→layers→light): **ultra-wide 20mm, high reverse-crane vantage pulling back and rising** over the whole colossal canyon. **Composition:** a small mid-air apex mark reserved on the upper-**right** third (rotated off the F1 left launch mark) with a clear diagonal of open sky and a dive-path dropping to the lower-left as the leading line; world-dominant deep space and huge sky negative space; horizon low so the sky dominates. **Foreground:** a near mesa-rim and a spire-tip close to the lens for parallax scale, overlapping the frame. **Midground:** the labyrinth of vermilion mesas and spires stepping back (parallax layer). **Background + atmosphere:** the turquoise river threading far below and distant ranges rendered markedly **hazier, cooler and lower-contrast** with layered dust-haze and cloud so the eye reads immense distance. **Light:** warm low sun soft key from high camera-back, broad soft fill, warm-near / cool-far separation, a gentle sun-glow near the apex. **Palette/grade:** vivid saturated vermilion, gold and turquoise against a magenta-gold sky — rich punchy colour, never dull or faded, natural skin tone preserved. one single cohesive photographed scene, not a panel/grid/collage; scene only, no subject; no text or labels; realistic rock textures and deep natural haze, not over-clean CGI.

[00:04–00:06] the camera cranes back and up to reveal the entire canyon and sky around her tiny soaring figure — easing back toward the mesa-lip opening for a loop as she starts her next dive. (Environment-dominant — she a small figure, the world immense.) # SS-03 · GIANT BLOOMS (Miniature in a Bloom-World) · 5 shots × 6s
**Logline:** Only a few inches tall, Aira explores a single sunlit flowerbed turned skyscraper-scale — towering
stalks, petals like sails, dewdrops like glass boulders — touches a colossal dewdrop that bursts into a shower of
rainbow light as the giant blooms glow, then rides a petal down before the whole bloom-world is revealed. Wonder,
cuteness, hyper-colour, inverted colossal scale.
**Arc (one place, explored):** THE REVEAL (hook · scale reveal) → EXPLORING (leaf-bridge · pollen-mote) → DEWDROP BURST (element · hero) → THE PETAL RIDE (trick · slide, POV/near-miss) → WORLD REVEAL (trick · reverse crane · loop).
*One coherent miniature bloom-world throughout — the same sunlit flowerbed of towering stalks, giant petals,
dewdrops, drifting pollen-motes and huge gentle pollinators — explored around one patch as she wanders it. **Element:**
a colossal dewdrop bursting into refracted rainbow light + the giant blooms glowing in a spreading wave, Frame 3 only.
**Trick shots:** the petal slide (F4) + reverse crane world-reveal (F5) — never the element's frame. **Hero:** Frame 3.
She is small-to-tiny in most frames; F1 & F5 environment-dominant.*
> **⚠️ SCALE IS THE WHOLE GAME (inverted):** the everyday world must read genuinely COLOSSAL because SHE is tiny —
> low **up-angles** gazing up the towering stalks, a dewdrop **bigger than her body**, petals arcing far overhead, a
> pollinator the size of a glider, **atmospheric depth-haze** down the flowerbed, parallax between foreground leaf,
> midground stalks and distant blooms. Every physic is REAL — real botanical sway, real water surface-tension and
> light-refraction in the dewdrops, real insect flight — with the one magical glow-bloom grounded in that reality
> (soft, premium, no cartoon sparkle). Real-time, no slow-mo. Lighting: warm sun raking *through* translucent petals.

## MASTER AUDIO (outside the frames — background music)
A 30-second custom cinematic score (5 clips × 6s) in self-resolving ~6-second phrases, trimmable at any phrase
boundary. Built on a twinkling celeste and glockenspiel, harp, warm strings, a light wordless choir and soft gentle
percussion — whimsical, wondrous, tender, magical. Opens (no fade-in) on a curious rising celeste figure. Arc: a
curious exploring open, a rising wonder, a sparkling shimmer-peak on the dewdrop burst, a joyful lift on the petal
ride, then a serene awe resolve on the world reveal for an invisible loop. Laid UNDER the diegetic — no spoken line;
the wordless choir lifts on the dewdrop burst only.

# SS-04 · SILK SWING (Web-Swing · Neon Megacity) · 5 shots × 6s
**Logline:** A graceful super-being dives off a glass tower, casts glowing silk-light lines and swings pendulum arcs
through a vivid neon-dusk megacity — spotting each anchor and timing each release — then soars up in a bloom of
light-ribbons and perches high as the whole city is revealed. Flowing power, electric colour, massive vertical scale.
**Arc (one place, explored):** THE DIVE-OFF (hook · the launch) → THE SWING (pendulum arc · her mechanics) → THE RELEASE (element · hero) → POV SWING (trick · first-person) → PERCH · WORLD REVEAL (trick · reverse crane · loop).
*One coherent neon-dusk megacity throughout — the same glass towers, electric signage, wet-reflective streets far
below and vivid twilight sky — she swings around and through it. **Element:** her cast light-line fanning into a
bloom of glowing ribbons and colour at the big release, Frame 3 only. **Trick shots:** first-person POV swing (F4) +
reverse crane world-reveal (F5) — never the element's frame. **Hero:** Frame 3. She reads clearly in the close beats;
F1 & F5 are environment-dominant (the city colossal around her).*
> **⚠️ SCALE + SWING BELIEVABILITY:** the swing is a REAL pendulum — she casts a line to a **high anchor ahead**, the
> line goes **taut**, she **accelerates through the bottom of the arc**, and **releases at the top of the upswing**
> into a ballistic soar, then casts the next line; her body is **active** throughout (leaning into the arc, legs
> tucked/trailing, gripping the line, aiming and timing the next anchor) — never a passive hang. The light-lines are
> grounded (taut, faintly elastic, casting real glow on wet glass) with the one magical bloom-beat. The skyscraper
> canyon must read genuinely COLOSSAL — high/low angles, her tiny against the towers, depth-haze, parallax, neon
> reflections. Real-time, no slow-mo.

## MASTER AUDIO (outside the frames — background music)
A 30-second custom cinematic score (5 clips × 6s) in self-resolving ~6-second phrases, trimmable at any phrase
boundary. Built on pulsing synth arpeggios, a driving beat, soaring strings, a cool wordless female vocal and a deep

# SS-05 · EMBERBORN (Winged Awakening · Cliff-Temple Dusk) · 5 shots × 6s
**Logline:** On an ancient cliff-top temple at storm-crimson dusk, Aira stands still — then embers ignite, vast
wings of living flame-and-colour erupt from her back, her eyes open ablaze, she unleashes a ground-cracking
shockwave and lifts off to soar away into the immense burning sky. A goddess awakening; fierce, vivid, colossal.
**Arc (one place, explored):** THE STILLNESS · IGNITION (hook) → WINGS OF FLAME (transform · trick) → EYES IGNITE (hero) → THE SHOCKWAVE (signature · she unleashes) → SKY ASCENT · WORLD REVEAL (trick · reverse crane · loop).
*One coherent cliff-temple plateau throughout — the same cracked stone, weathered pillars, vast range below and
dramatic crimson-violet sky — the awakening unfolds in this one place. **Transformation concept — TWO subject-state
sheets:** `@subject_base` (before) and `@subject_awakened` (winged, flame-eyes). The magical transformation is the
through-line; **signature** = the ground shockwave (F4). **Hero:** Frame 3 (eyes ignite). She reads clearly in the
close beats; F1 & F5 are environment-dominant.*
> **⚠️ SCALE · BELIEVABILITY · IDENTITY:** the transformation is the impossible beat but every physic is REAL —
> **real fire** (heat-shimmer, ember-lift, light cast on stone and skin, drifting ash, smoke), **wings of flame with
> real fire-dynamics + a believable feather-of-flame structure and downdraft**, a **real ground shockwave / crater /
> debris** on true arcs, and real wing-borne lift; premium, NO cheap cartoon VFX or plastic CGI. The plateau + range
> + sky must read COLOSSAL (low angles, her tiny in F1/F5, depth-haze, wings that exceed the frame). **Identity-safe:**
> her face, bone structure and eye SHAPE never change — the "flame eyes" are an inner molten flame-**glow** added
> within her amber-hazel irises, and only her gaze + soft expression animate. Real-time, no slow-mo.

boundary. Built on a low sacred drone, a rising wordless choir, thunderous taiko and frame-drums, huge brass and
soaring strings and a deep sub — sacred, fierce, ascendant. Opens (no fade-in) on a hushed drone and a single choir
voice. Arc: a still, charged open, a swelling choir-and-drum surge on the wing-eruption, a held breath on the eyes,
a thunderous brass-and-drum hit on the shockwave, then a soaring triumphant ascent that resolves for an invisible
loop. Laid UNDER the diegetic — no spoken line; the wordless choir peaks on the wings and the ascent.

# SS-06 · AURORA DANCER (Living-Light Dance · Frozen Lake Night) · 5 shots × 6s
**Logline:** Night on a mirror-still frozen lake ringed by snow peaks; Aira dances and the northern lights descend as
ribbons of living light that answer her every move, swirling around her before she releases them back to the vast
starry sky. Ethereal, electric, graceful — colour born from light, not saturation.
**Arc (one place, explored):** THE IGNITION (hook · silhouette-to-colour) → FIRST RIBBON (the light answers her) → THE VORTEX (element · hero) → LIGHT IN MOTION (trick) → RELEASE · WORLD REVEAL (trick · reverse crane · loop).
*One coherent frozen-lake night throughout — the same black mirror-ice, ringing snow peaks, starry sky and living
aurora — she dances in this one place. **Element:** the aurora drawn down into a swirling ribbon-vortex around her,
Frame 3 only. **Trick shots:** the braiding light-trails in motion (F4) + reverse crane world-reveal (F5) — never the
element's frame. **Hero:** Frame 3. She is a small graceful figure in an immense glowing world; F1 & F5 are
environment-dominant.*
> **⚠️ SCALE · BELIEVABILITY · VIVID-VIA-LIGHT:** the aurora, stars, mirror-ice reflections and cold breath-mist are
> all REAL; the magical beat is **ribbons of aurora flowing down** to braid around her like luminous silk-plasma,
> casting real coloured light on the ice and her skin (ethereal, grounded — no cartoon sparkle). The vast sky, the
> aurora filling the heavens, the ringing peaks and the mirror-lake must read COLOSSAL (she tiny in the wides,
> depth-haze, huge reflections). Vivid colour comes from the **aurora LIGHT** — her gown and skin stay near-neutral
> so the colour reads and her skin stays natural. Graceful **real-time** dance, full per-limb, no slow-mo.

## MASTER AUDIO (outside the frames — background music)
A 30-second custom cinematic score (5 clips × 6s) in self-resolving ~6-second phrases, trimmable at any phrase
boundary. Built on celestial synth pads, a glass-harp and crystal bells, an ethereal wordless female vocal, soft
strings, a gentle heartbeat-pulse and a deep sub — ethereal, shimmering, transcendent. Opens (no fade-in) on a hushed
starlit pad and a single high vocal. Arc: a still starlit open, a swelling shimmer as the sky ignites, a flowing
dance groove, a soaring crystalline peak on the vortex, then a serene resolve on the world reveal for an invisible
loop. Laid UNDER the diegetic — no spoken line; the wordless vocal lifts on the vortex only.

# SS-07 · LIGHTNING RUNNER (Super-Speed · Storm Coast Dusk) · 5 shots × 6s
**Logline:** A super-speed runner explodes into a sprint along a vast storm-dusk coast, blazing across the wet sand
and out over the sea surface trailing arcs of lightning-light, breaks a sonic vapour-cone in a burst of electricity,
then skids to a halt as her glowing trail arcs across the whole coast. Electric, kinetic, colossal scale.
**Arc (one place, explored):** THE BOLT-START (hook · the launch/ground-skim) → THE RUN (parallel speed · her sprint) → SONIC BURST (element · hero) → WATER RUN (trick · running on the sea) → SKID · WORLD REVEAL (trick · reverse crane · loop).
*One coherent storm-dusk coast throughout — the same wet mirror-sand, shallow tidal sea, distant headlands and vast
dramatic sky — she blazes around and across it. **Element:** the sonic vapour-cone + radial lightning-shockwave
burst, Frame 3 only. **Trick shots:** running on the sea surface (F4) + reverse crane world-reveal (F5) — never the
element's frame. **Hero:** Frame 3. She reads clearly in the close beats; F1 & F5 are environment-dominant.*
> **⚠️ SCALE + SUPER-SPEED BELIEVABILITY:** super-speed is shown by the **WORLD streaking past** in real motion-blur
> (NOT slow-mo — she moves fast, the world smears); real sprint biomechanics (driving arms, high knees, forward lean,
> explosive strides, a hard lean and hand-skim on turns); lightning as **real electrical arcs** crackling off her
> with true light-cast; displaced air, wet-sand spray and a near-sonic **vapour-cone + shockwave**; running-on-water
> via speed (real surface-tension skim, twin spray-fans, spreading rings). The coast + sea + storm sky must read
> COLOSSAL (she tiny in the wides, her trail carved across it, depth-haze). Vivid from the **electric lightning +
> storm-sunset light**, skin natural. Real-time, no slow-mo.

## MASTER AUDIO (outside the frames — background music)
A 30-second custom cinematic score (5 clips × 6s) in self-resolving ~6-second phrases, trimmable at any phrase
boundary. Built on a pulsing synth bass, fast driving percussion, crackling electric textures, soaring strings, a
fierce wordless female vocal and a deep sub — electric, driving, exhilarating. Opens (no fade-in) on an explosive
percussive-and-electric hit. Arc: a detonating start, an accelerating drive, a build-and-crack peak on the sonic
burst, a euphoric surge on the water run, then a settling triumphant resolve for an invisible loop. Laid UNDER the
diegetic — no spoken line; the fierce wordless vocal lifts on the sonic burst only.

# SS-08 · THE WHITE STALLION (Dawn-Mist Bond) · 5 shots × 6s
**Logline:** In a vast wildflower meadow at misty dawn, a majestic white stallion emerges from the fog; Aira meets it,
strokes its face and rests her brow to its, and they move together as the sun breaks and golden light-motes drift —
a tender, wordless bond. Grounded, ethereal, awe-struck.
**Arc (one place, explored):** THE MIST (hook · fog parts) → THE APPROACH → THE BOND (element · hero) → TOGETHER (trick · the circling gallop) → SUNRISE · WORLD REVEAL (trick · reverse crane · loop).
*One coherent misty dawn meadow throughout — the same wildflower field, lone oak, distant blue mountains and soft
sunrise — the meeting unfolds in this one place. **Element:** drifting golden dawn light-motes as the mist glows and
the sun breaks, Frame 3 only. **Trick shots:** the circling gallop (F4) + reverse crane world-reveal (F5) — never the
element's frame. **Hero:** Frame 3. Grounded realism throughout (real horse, real mist, real dawn light); she reads
clearly in the close beats, F1 & F5 environment-dominant.*
> **⚠️ REALISM + SCALE + VIVID-VIA-CONTRAST:** maximum believability — a real horse (true anatomy, gait, breath,
> mane and muscle, natural behaviour: nuzzle, snort, canter), real ground-fog swirling and lifting, real low dawn
> light and dew; the only "magic" is gentle **golden light-motes** drifting in the warming mist (grounded, no cartoon
> sparkle). Scale is atmospheric — the vast misty meadow, big sunrise sky and distant mountains (she small in the
> wides, depth-haze). Vivid comes from **her rich emerald gown glowing against the pale-gold mist** and the sunrise —
> contrast, not blanket saturation; skin natural. Real-time, no slow-mo.

## MASTER AUDIO (outside the frames — background music)
A 30-second custom cinematic score (5 clips × 6s) in self-resolving ~6-second phrases, trimmable at any phrase
boundary. Built on warm strings, a felt piano, a soft harp, a tender wordless female vocal and light woodwinds —
tender, ethereal, awe-struck. Opens (no fade-in) on a hushed misty string-and-piano figure. Arc: a still, misty
open, a tender meeting, a soaring warm peak on the bond, a joyful lift on the gallop, then a serene sunrise resolve
for an invisible loop. Laid UNDER the diegetic — no spoken line; the wordless vocal lifts on the bond only.

# SS-09 · A MILLION BLOOMS (Super-Bloom Cliff) · 5 shots × 6s
**Logline:** On a high clifftop above a vast valley erupting into a super-bloom to the horizon, Aira walks through the
flower-sea, sweeps her hand and sends a wave of blooming rippling across the valley as petals lift into a great swirl,
before the whole immense flower-world is revealed. Joyful, awe-struck, hyper-colour.
**Arc (one place, explored):** THE OVERLOOK (hook · scale reveal) → WALKING THE BLOOMS → THE BLOOM-WAVE (element · hero) → PETAL LIFT (trick) → VALLEY REVEAL (trick · reverse crane · loop).
*One coherent super-bloom valley throughout — the same clifftop, flower-sea, distant mountains and vivid sky — she
explores this one overlook. **Element:** a wave of blooming rippling across the valley + petals lifting into a swirl,
Frame 3 only. **Trick shots:** the petal lift (F4) + reverse crane valley-reveal (F5) — never the element's frame.
**Hero:** Frame 3. She reads clearly in the close beats; F1 & F5 environment-dominant, the flower-sea the vivid star.*
> **⚠️ REALISM + SCALE + VIVID:** real flowers and botany, real wind stirring and lifting petals, real light; the only
> magic is the gentle **bloom-wave** (flowers genuinely opening in a spreading ripple) and wind-lifted petals
> (grounded, premium, no cartoon sparkle). The valley must read COLOSSAL — a flower-sea to the horizon, distant
> mountains, huge sky, depth-haze, she small in the wides. Vivid comes from the **flower-sea itself** (magenta,
> gold, coral, violet); she wears soft blush-cream so the blooms stay the vivid hero and she reads in clear
> figure-to-ground. Real-time, no slow-mo.

## MASTER AUDIO (outside the frames — background music)
A 30-second custom cinematic score (5 clips × 6s) in self-resolving ~6-second phrases, trimmable at any phrase
boundary. Built on soaring strings, a warm piano, a bright harp, a light wordless female vocal, gentle percussion and
woodwinds — lush, uplifting, awe-struck. Opens (no fade-in) on an awe-struck rising string figure. Arc: an awe-struck
open on the vista, a joyful walk, a soaring peak on the bloom-wave, a lifting swirl on the petals, then a serene vista
resolve for an invisible loop. Laid UNDER the diegetic — no spoken line; the wordless vocal lifts on the bloom-wave only.

# SS-10 · PETAL VORTEX (Living-Petal Dance · Ruined Temple Dusk) · 5 shots × 6s
**Logline:** In a vast ancient temple ruin on a high plateau at golden-magenta dusk, Aira dances and a colossal storm
of glowing petals answers her — she draws it into a towering spiralling vortex around her that bursts into a bloom,
before the whole grand ruin and petal-storm are revealed. Graceful, hyper-colour, awe-struck.
**Arc (one place, explored):** THE GATHERING (hook · element ignition) → FIRST STREAM (petals answer her) → THE VORTEX (element · hero) → PETALS IN MOTION (trick) → RELEASE · WORLD REVEAL (trick · reverse crane · loop).
*One coherent temple-ruin plateau throughout — the same weathered stone, giant blossom trees, vast dusk sky and
swirling petals — she dances in this one place. **Element:** the petals drawn into a colossal spiralling vortex-tower
around her that bursts into a bloom, Frame 3 only. **Trick shots:** the petals braiding in motion (F4) + reverse
crane world-reveal (F5) — never the element's frame. **Hero:** Frame 3. She reads clearly in the close beats; F1 &
F5 are environment-dominant.*
> **⚠️ SCALE · BELIEVABILITY · VIVID:** real petals swept on real wind in a true vortex, but **answering her dance**
> (the magical beat, grounded — no cartoon sparkle); a faint glow on the petals reads as warm dusk backlight. The
> temple ruin, giant blossom trees, towering petal-vortex and vast sky must read COLOSSAL (she small in the wides,
> depth-haze, the vortex towering above her). Vivid comes from the **petal-storm itself** (magenta, coral, gold,
> violet); she wears soft dusty-rose so the petals stay the vivid hero and she reads in clear figure-to-ground.
> Graceful **real-time** dance, full per-limb, no slow-mo.

## MASTER AUDIO (outside the frames — background music)
A 30-second custom cinematic score (5 clips × 6s) in self-resolving ~6-second phrases, trimmable at any phrase
boundary. Built on sweeping lush strings, a harp, a felt piano, a warm wordless female vocal and gentle percussion —
romantic, sweeping, awe-struck. Opens (no fade-in) on a rising harp-and-string figure. Arc: a gathering open, a
tender flowing build, a soaring swell on the vortex-bloom, a lifting turn on the petals in motion, then a serene
resolve on the reveal for an invisible loop. Laid UNDER the diegetic — no spoken line; the wordless vocal lifts on
the vortex only.

# SS-11 · SONG OF LIGHT (Voice-as-Light · Cathedral Cavern Night) · 5 shots × 6s
**Logline:** In a colossal natural stone cathedral-cavern with a mirror-still pool, Aira lifts a wordless song and
her voice becomes visible — waves and ribbons of vivid light rippling out to fill the darkness, blooming across the
water and the towering stone, before the whole immense cavern is revealed aglow. Synesthetic, ethereal, radiant.
**Arc (one place, explored):** THE FIRST NOTE (hook · silhouette-to-colour) → RIPPLES (the light answers her voice) → THE BLOOM (element · hero) → LIGHT IN MOTION (trick) → RESONANCE · WORLD REVEAL (trick · reverse crane · loop).
*One coherent cathedral-cavern throughout — the same towering dark stone, mirror pool, moonlight-shafts and rising
light — her wordless song fills this one place. **Element:** a great sustained note sending a colossal bloom of
light-waves radiating out to fill the cavern, Frame 3 only. **Trick shots:** the light-waves braiding in motion (F4)
+ reverse crane world-reveal (F5) — never the element's frame. **Hero:** Frame 3. She reads clearly in the close
beats; F1 & F5 environment-dominant. SILENT reel — her song is a WORDLESS vocal, no lyrics.*
> **⚠️ SCALE · BELIEVABILITY · VIVID-VIA-LIGHT:** the cavern, stone, mirror-pool and moonlight are all REAL; the
> magical beat is her **voice becoming visible waves of light** — concentric ripples and ribbons that expand on real
> wave-physics, cast real coloured light on the stone and water and reflect in the pool (ethereal, grounded — no
> cartoon sparkle). The colossal cavern must read ENORMOUS (she tiny in the wides, towering stone, depth-haze, huge
> reflections). Vivid comes from the **light-waves** against the dark stone — her gown and skin stay near-neutral so
> the colour reads and skin stays natural. Real-time, no slow-mo. She sings WORDLESSLY — never any lyrics.

## MASTER AUDIO (outside the frames — background music)
A 30-second custom cinematic score (5 clips × 6s) in self-resolving ~6-second phrases, trimmable at any phrase
boundary. Built around a soaring **wordless** female vocal (no words), a glass-harp and crystal bells, celestial
pads, soft strings, a gentle heartbeat-pulse and a deep sub — ethereal, resonant, transcendent. Opens (no fade-in)
on a single hushed wordless vocal tone in the dark. Arc: a still open on the first note, a swelling shimmer as the
light ripples, a soaring crystalline peak on the bloom, a flowing lift on the light in motion, then a serene resolve
on the reveal for an invisible loop. Purely wordless throughout — no lyric.

Vivid, saturated, true colours throughout (carried by the light).
**Look-swatch prompt** (the 3rd ingredient · prop-less concept · generate once · reuse in every frame · one image · plain background · no text/labels): a clean colour-and-light swatch of the reel's look — deep near-black stone and a black mirror-pool with cool moonlight, and vivid waves of teal, violet, magenta and gold light rippling across the dark with real light-cast and reflections, and a midnight-blue gown catching the colour; rich saturated light against dark neutral ground, natural skin tone; no text or labels rendered.
**Environment prompt** (base-plate — lens→composition→layers→light): **ultra-wide 20mm wide, low vantage across the black pool, horizon centred so the pool mirrors the stone.** **Composition:** a small ground-contact mark reserved centre on the broad flat stone stage for a tiny figure (eyeline lifting into the vault), soaring columns and the vast pool as world-dominant negative space above and below; cool moonlight-shafts lead the eye up; dominant light a cool moonlight key from a high opening camera-back-left. **Foreground:** the vast mirror-still black pool close to the lens perfectly reflecting the stone, drifting mist, overlapping the frame for parallax. **Midground:** the broad flat stone stage at the pool's edge where the tiny figure stands. **Background + atmosphere:** soaring dark rock columns and vaulted stone rising into shadow, rendered **hazier, cooler and lower-contrast** with cool shafts of moonlight from high openings and drifting mist (aerial perspective = immense cathedral scale). **Light:** cool moonlight key from high camera-back-left, deep shadow fill, the first vivid light-bloom washing from the figure — vivid-via-light against near-black stone. **Palette/grade:** deep near-black stone with vivid teal, violet, magenta and gold light-waves — rich punchy colour from the light, never dull or faded, natural skin tone preserved. one single cohesive photographed scene, not a panel/grid/collage; scene only, no subject; no text or labels; realistic wet-stone, mirror-pool and mist textures, not over-clean CGI.

WIDE on an **ultra-wide 20mm (deep focus)**, **one continuous slow push-in as the first light blooms** — she composited **tiny on the reserved centred stage-mark** (crop tiny-in-wide, facing front/away lifting the note), dwarfed by the colossal vault and its mirror below, the cool moonlight key from **high camera-back-left matching the plate** with the first light-bloom washing from her; deep focus, parallax between the near mirror-pool, her tiny figure and the haze-softened far columns. [00:00–00:02] **HOOK — silhouette-to-colour:** she stands a small silhouette in the dark cavern lit only by a shaft of moonlight — then she lifts a soft **wordless note** and a **ripple of vivid light blooms out** from her, washing across the black pool and up the towering stone, lighting the dark; the scroll-stopper. # SS-12 · BUTTERFLY METAMORPHOSIS (Winged Rebirth · Enchanted Garden) · 5 shots × 6s
**Logline:** In a lush enchanted garden of towering flowers at golden hour, a glowing chrysalis of light cracks and
Aira emerges, her vivid stained-glass butterfly wings unfurling and blooming into colour, before she lifts on a
gentle flutter into the sunlit garden and the whole world is revealed. Tender, wondrous, hyper-colour.
**Arc (one place, explored):** THE CHRYSALIS (hook · light ignition) → THE EMERGENCE (transform · trick) → WINGS UNFURL (element · hero) → FIRST FLIGHT (trick) → GARDEN · WORLD REVEAL (trick · reverse crane · loop).
*One coherent enchanted garden throughout — the same towering flowers, hanging chrysalis-branch, golden light and
drifting spores and butterflies — the rebirth unfolds in this one place. **Transformation concept — TWO subject-state
sheets:** `@subject_base` (before) and `@subject_winged` (vivid butterfly wings). **Element:** the wings unfurling
and blooming into full stained-glass colour, Frame 3 only. **Trick shots:** the first flutter-flight (F4) + reverse
crane world-reveal (F5). **Hero:** Frame 3. She reads clearly in the close beats; F1 & F5 environment-dominant.*
> **⚠️ SCALE · BELIEVABILITY · VIVID:** the garden, giant flora, chrysalis and real butterflies are believable; the
> magic is the **glowing chrysalis of light** and her **stained-glass butterfly wings** unfurling with true
> butterfly-emergence physics (wings first crumpled and soft, then pumping full and drying, catching light like
> real wing-membrane — grounded, no cartoon sparkle). Flight is a **gentle, buoyant flutter/glide** appropriate to
> huge delicate wings — NOT fast flapping. Giant flora, wings that exceed the frame, depth-haze and a world reveal
> give COLOSSAL scale. Vivid comes from the **stained-glass wings** (magenta, violet, gold, teal); her gown stays
> soft so the wings are the hero. Identity-safe: face + eye shape unchanged. Real-time, no slow-mo.

## MASTER AUDIO (outside the frames — background music)
A 30-second custom cinematic score (5 clips × 6s) in self-resolving ~6-second phrases, trimmable at any phrase
boundary. Built on a twinkling celeste, harp, warm strings, a tender wordless female vocal and soft gentle
percussion — magical, tender, wondrous. Opens (no fade-in) on a hushed shimmering celeste. Arc: a still, glowing
open, a cracking shimmer of emergence, a soaring bloom as the wings unfurl, a light lifting flutter on first flight,
then a serene resolve on the garden reveal for an invisible loop. Laid UNDER the diegetic — no spoken line; the
wordless vocal lifts on the wing-unfurl only.

# SS-13 · WATER WEAVER (Water Command · Turquoise Canyon Lagoon) · 5 shots × 6s
**Logline:** On a still turquoise lagoon in a vast canyon at golden-magenta sunset, Aira raises her arms and the water
rises with her — ribbons coiling into a colossal serpent of living water that spirals around her, before she sweeps
it into a great arcing wave and lets it fall back in a shimmer of rain. Powerful, fluid, vivid, immense.
**Arc (one place, explored):** THE FIRST DRAW (hook · surface-break rise) → WATER RIBBONS (she weaves) → THE WATER SERPENT (element · hero) → THE WAVE (trick) → RELEASE · WORLD REVEAL (trick · reverse crane · loop).
*One coherent turquoise canyon-lagoon throughout — the same still water, vermilion cliffs, sunset sky and rising
water — she commands it from one place. **Element:** the water drawn up into a colossal towering serpent of living
water coiling around her, Frame 3 only. **Trick shots:** the great sweeping wave (F4) + reverse crane world-reveal
(F5) — never the element's frame. **Hero:** Frame 3. She reads clearly in the close beats; F1 & F5 environment-dominant.*
> **⚠️ SCALE · BELIEVABILITY · VIVID:** real water throughout — true cohesion, weight, spray, droplets, refraction and
> light through it, rainbow mist — shaped by her will (the magical beat, grounded — no plastic CGI or floaty
> nonsense). The lagoon, vermilion cliffs, colossal water-serpent (towering many times her height) and vast sky must
> read ENORMOUS (she tiny in the wides, depth-haze, the serpent exceeding the frame). Vivid comes from the **turquoise
> water + sunset light + refracted rainbow spray**; skin natural. She commands the water with graceful, deliberate
> gestures. Real-time, no slow-mo.

## MASTER AUDIO (outside the frames — background music)
A 30-second custom cinematic score (5 clips × 6s) in self-resolving ~6-second phrases, trimmable at any phrase
boundary. Built on flowing strings, soft taiko and water-percussion, a fluid wordless female vocal, harp and shimmer
textures and a deep sub — powerful, fluid, ethereal. Opens (no fade-in) on a still shimmer and a low swell. Arc: a
calm still open, a rising flowing build, a powerful swell on the water-serpent, a sweeping surge on the wave, then a
serene resolve on the release for an invisible loop. Laid UNDER the diegetic — no spoken line; the wordless vocal
lifts on the serpent only.

# SS-14 · SEA OF CLOUDS (Cloud-Ocean Summit · Sunrise) · 5 shots × 6s
**Logline:** On a high mountain ridge above a vast ocean of clouds at sunrise, Aira walks the summit, sweeps her arm
and parts the cloud-sea in a great glowing rift as it surges and swirls around her, before the whole boundless
cloud-ocean and dawn sky are revealed. Serene, awe-struck, immense.
**Arc (one place, explored):** THE SUMMIT (hook · silhouette-to-colour) → WALKING THE RIDGE → THE PARTING (element · hero) → THE CLOUD-SURGE (trick) → CLOUD-OCEAN · WORLD REVEAL (trick · reverse crane · loop).
*One coherent summit-above-clouds throughout — the same ridge, cloud-ocean, distant island-peaks and sunrise sky —
she crosses this one place. **Element:** the cloud-sea parting in a great glowing rift at her gesture, Frame 3 only.
**Trick shots:** the cloud-surge swirling around her (F4) + reverse crane world-reveal (F5). **Hero:** Frame 3. She
small in the wides; F1 & F5 environment-dominant.*
> **⚠️ SCALE · BELIEVABILITY · VIVID:** real clouds, real ridge-rock, real dawn light and wind; the magic is the
> cloud-sea **parting and surging at her gesture** (grounded cloud/mist behaviour — no floaty nonsense, she never
> stands on solid cloud). The cloud-ocean to the horizon, distant island-peaks, huge sky and depth-haze give
> COLOSSAL scale (she tiny in the wides). Vivid comes from the **sunrise light flooding the cloud-sea** (gold, coral,
> magenta); her gown stays soft so the sky is the hero. Real-time, no slow-mo.

## MASTER AUDIO (outside the frames — background music)
A 30-second custom cinematic score (5 clips × 6s) in self-resolving ~6-second phrases, trimmable at any phrase
boundary. Built on warm swelling strings, a felt piano, a soft wordless choir and light woodwinds — serene, majestic,
awe-struck. Opens (no fade-in) on a hushed dawn string figure. Arc: a still summit open, a walking lift, a soaring
swell on the parting, a lifting surge as the clouds rise, then a serene resolve on the reveal for an invisible loop.
Laid UNDER the diegetic — no spoken line; the wordless choir lifts on the parting only.

**Logline:** In a surreal moonlit garden beneath a giant low moon, Aira wanders among night-blooming flowers; she

the whole dream-garden is revealed aglow. Dreamlike, tender, vivid-cool.
**Arc (one place, explored):** THE MOONRISE (hook · lights-on) → WANDERING → THE NIGHT-BLOOM (element · hero) → THE GLOWING POND (trick) → DREAM-GARDEN · WORLD REVEAL (trick · reverse crane · loop).
*One coherent moonlit dream-garden throughout — the same giant low moon, glowing night-blooms, still koi-pond, arched
bridge and willows — she wanders this one place. **Element:** the moonflowers opening and glowing in a spreading wave
at her touch + light-motes lifting, Frame 3 only. **Trick shots:** the glowing koi-pond (F4) + reverse crane
world-reveal (F5). **Hero:** Frame 3. She reads clearly in the close beats; F1 & F5 environment-dominant.*

# SS-16 · STARFALL (Colour-Wish Meteor Shower · Night Dunes) · 5 shots × 6s
**Logline:** On a vast dune-field beneath a huge starry sky streaked by a meteor shower, Aira lifts her finger toward
the falling stars and the ones she touches burst into ribbons of vivid colour across the heavens, before she catches
a fallen star of light and the whole colour-painted sky is revealed. Wondrous, celestial, vivid.
**Arc (one place, explored):** THE STARFALL (hook · element ignition) → THE REACH (the twist begins) → COLOUR-FALL (element · hero) → THE FALLEN STAR (trick) → PAINTED SKY · WORLD REVEAL (trick · reverse crane · loop).
*One coherent night dune-field throughout — the same vast dunes, huge starry sky and meteor shower — she stands and
wishes in this one place. **The twist / element:** she lifts her finger toward the streaking meteors and the ones she
touches **release ribbons of vivid colour** across the sky, Frame 3 the peak. **Trick shots:** catching a fallen star
(F4) + reverse crane world-reveal (F5). **Hero:** Frame 3. She small in the wides; F1 & F5 environment-dominant.*
> **⚠️ SCALE · BELIEVABILITY · VIVID-VIA-LIGHT:** real dunes, a real deep starfield and real streaking meteors; the
> magic is that **the meteors she points to bloom into vivid colour-ribbons** and a fallen star drifts to her hand
> (grounded light, no cartoon sparkle). The vast dune-field, the huge sky and depth-haze give COLOSSAL scale (she
> tiny in the wides). Vivid comes from the **colour-releasing meteors** against the dark starry sky (vivid-via-light);
> her gown near-neutral so the colour reads, skin natural. Real-time, no slow-mo.

## MASTER AUDIO (outside the frames — background music)
A 30-second custom cinematic score (5 clips × 6s) in self-resolving ~6-second phrases, trimmable at any phrase
boundary. Built on soft celestial synth pads, a twinkling glockenspiel, a harp, gentle strings, a wordless female
vocal and a deep sub — wondrous, celestial, tender. Opens (no fade-in) on a hushed pad and a single twinkle. Arc: a
still starlit open, a rising twinkle as she reaches, a soaring shimmer-peak on the colour-fall, a tender lift as she
catches the star, then a serene resolve on the reveal for an invisible loop. Laid UNDER the diegetic — no spoken
line; the wordless vocal lifts on the colour-fall only.

**Logline:** In a sun-drenched silk-draped chamber opening to a blooming garden, Aira lifts the hero flacon, releases
it, and a ribbon of glowing scent-light swirls and blooms into petals of light around her, before she lands a serene
radiant hero with the bottle. Dreamy, sensory, luxe — gentle throughout.
**Arc (one place, explored):** THE FLACON (hook · light-flare) → THE TOUCH → THE SCENT (element · hero) → THE SWIRL (trick) → THE HERO · REVEAL (loop).
*One coherent silk-and-garden chamber throughout — the same billowing sheer curtains, marble, drifting petals and
golden light — the scent film unfolds in this one place. **This is a Fragrance Ad:** motion stays GENTLE (soft
drifting movement — no action), the **object sheet is the HERO FLACON**. **Hero:** Frame 3 (the scent). Real skin
texture, skin tone protected.*
> **⚠️ BEAUTY REALISM + GENTLE MOTION:** her face and skin are hero — **real skin texture, natural glow, never
> plastic**; identity exact. Per the fragrance rule, all movement is **soft and gentle** (drifting silk, a slow
> turn, a raised bottle — no big action). The magic is the **scent made visible** — a ribbon of glowing scent-light
> that swirls and blooms into petals of light (grounded, premium, no cartoon sparkle). Vivid comes from the **golden
> light + blooming garden + the flacon**; skin natural, the chamber a co-star. Real-time, no slow-mo; a soft loop.

boundary. Built on soft lush strings, a harp, a felt piano, an airy wordless female vocal and a delicate pulse —
dreamy, sensual, elegant. Opens (no fade-in) on a soft harp-and-string shimmer. Arc: a dreamy sunlit open, a tender
approach, a soaring bloom on the scent-release, a gentle swirl, then a serene resolve on the hero for an invisible
loop. Laid UNDER the diegetic — no spoken line; the airy wordless vocal lifts on the scent only.

# SS-20 · HAIR FILM (Glossy Hair Hero) · 5 shots × 6s
**Logline:** In a warm editorial set with a soft wind, Aira turns and moves through glossy, healthy hair in motion —
a full run-through, a radiant real-time hair-flip fanning into a shining arc, the hero hair-oil, and a landed
radiant hero. A premium hair film — shine, movement, health. Real-time, no slow-mo.
**Arc (one place, explored):** THE REVEAL (hook · hair-flip) → HAIR IN MOTION → THE FLIP (hero) → THE RUN-THROUGH · PRODUCT (trick) → THE FINAL HERO (loop).
*One coherent editorial set throughout — the same warm colour-blocked backdrop, soft wind and beauty light — the
hair film unfolds in this one place. **This is a Hair Ad:** it carries REAL hair-in-motion (turns, flips,
run-through) at natural speed — NO slow-mo; the **object sheet is the HERO HAIR PRODUCT**. **Hero:** Frame 3 (the
flip). Real skin texture, skin tone protected; the hair is the star.*
> **⚠️ BEAUTY REALISM + HAIR MOTION:** real skin and **real, healthy, glossy hair with believable movement, weight
> and shine** (individual strands catching light, natural bounce and fall — never plastic or gummy); identity exact.
> Hair movement is **real-time / natural speed** (a real flip and fall — NO slow-mo). Vivid comes from the **warm
> editorial set + the glossy hair-shine**; skin natural, clean figure-to-ground. A clean loop.

## MASTER AUDIO (outside the frames — background music)
A 30-second custom cinematic score (5 clips × 6s) in self-resolving ~6-second phrases, trimmable at any phrase
boundary. Built on a sleek confident beat, warm bass, a bright string swell, a chic wordless female vocal and crisp
percussion — glossy, premium, feel-good. Opens (no fade-in) on a punchy beat-and-string hit. Arc: a confident
reveal open, a flowing motion build, a bright lift on the flip, a smooth run-through, then a landed radiant hero
resolve for an invisible loop. Laid UNDER the diegetic — no spoken line; the wordless vocal lifts on the flip only.

**Set totals:** 20 concepts · 19 × 5-frame + 1 × 4-frame (SS-01) = **99 video clips × 6s**. Sheets reused across
frames per concept; only the environment still + video prompt change per frame. Every reel: a deliberate HOOK on
Frame 1, engineered massive/inverted scale where it applies, a cute flower-like identity-safe subject with a
per-frame environment-reactive expression written into every video prompt, vivid colour (skin protected), a distinct
heavily-detailed hairstyle + rotating headwear, back & POV shots spread across the set, and TWO audio blocks (Master
score + beat-synced diegetic). Real-time motion, grounded physics, seamless loops, 9:16, silent.
